export type AppBarExtraComponentsHook = () => AppBarExtraComponentsDefinition;

export const HeaderContent = () => {
  const { /*i18n, */ menuOrientation } = useConfig();

  const { service: appBarExtraComponentsHook } =
    useTrackService<AppBarExtraComponentsHook>(AppBarExtraComponentsFilter);
  const appBarExtraComponentsDefinition: AppBarExtraComponentsDefinition | undefined = appBarExtraComponentsHook?.();
  const appBarExtraSpaceClaim: number = appBarExtraComponentsDefinition?.spaceRequired ?? 1;

  const { downLG, isMenuOrientationHorizontal } = useLayoutHelper();

  return (
    <Grid container alignItems="center" sx={{ flexGrow: 1 }}>
      {isMenuOrientationHorizontal && (
        <Grid size={2}>
          <DrawerHeader open={true} />
        </Grid>
      )}
      {isMenuOrientationHorizontal && (
        <Grid size={8 - appBarExtraSpaceClaim}>
          <HorizontalBar />
        </Grid>
      )}
      {(downLG || menuOrientation !== MenuOrientation.HORIZONTAL) && <Grid size={10 - appBarExtraSpaceClaim} />}
      <Grid size={2 + appBarExtraSpaceClaim}>
        <Grid container justifyContent="flex-end">
          {appBarExtraComponentsDefinition && <appBarExtraComponentsDefinition.Component />}
          <Customization />
          <HeaderProfile />
        </Grid>
      </Grid>
    </Grid>
  );
};

import Box from '@mui/material/Box';
import Container from '@mui/material/Container';
import { useTheme } from '@mui/material/styles';
import useScrollTrigger from '@mui/material/useScrollTrigger';
import type { ReactElement } from 'react';
import { cloneElement } from 'react';
import { useConfig } from '~/hooks';
import { Navigation } from '~/layout/Drawer/DrawerContent/Navigation';

interface ElevationScrollProps {
  children: ReactElement;
  window?: Window | Node;
}

function ElevationScroll({ children, window }: ElevationScrollProps) {
  // const theme = useTheme();
  const trigger = useScrollTrigger({
    disableHysteresis: true,
    threshold: 0,
    target: window!,
  });

  return cloneElement(children, {
    elevation: trigger ? 4 : 0,
  } as any);
}

export const HorizontalBar = () => {
  const theme = useTheme();
  const { container } = useConfig();

  return (
    <Container maxWidth={container ? 'xl' : false}>
      <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
        <Navigation />
      </Box>
    </Container>
  );
};

[7498] TOOLRESULT: ## AppBar Extra Components

### Purpose

Add custom components to the application's AppBar (top navigation bar), such as notification bells, user menus, theme toggles, or quick action buttons.

### Basic Implementation

```typescript
// src/custom/components/NotificationBell.tsx
import { FC } from 'react';
import { IconButton, Badge } from '@mui/material';
import { Notifications as NotificationsIcon } from '@mui/icons-material';

export const NotificationBell: FC = () => {
  const notificationCount = 4; // Replace with actual logic

  const handleClick = () => {
    // Open notifications panel
    console.log('Open notifications');
  };

  return (
    <IconButton color="inherit" onClick={handleClick}>
      <Badge badgeContent={notificationCount} color="error">
        <NotificationsIcon />
      </Badge>
    </IconButton>
  );
};
```

### Registration

The AppBar accepts exactly one extra component, registered as a Pandino service under `CUSTOM_VISUAL_ELEMENT_INTERFACE_KEY` (value `'CustomVisualElement'`, exported from `~/custom`) with the service property `component: 'AppBarExtraComponents'`. The service is an `AppBarExtraComponentsHook` (from `~/layout/Header/HeaderContent`): a function returning `{ Component, spaceRequired? }`. `spaceRequired` (default 1) is the grid space the component claims in addition to the default header content.

```typescript

// Sellers' products (Level 8) fill more than one page of the back office tables: show the largest page size.
export async function showAllRows(page: Page) {
  await expect(page.getByText(/Rows per page/)).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
  const size = page.getByRole('combobox', { name: /Rows per page/ });
  await size.click();
  await page.getByRole('option').last().click();
  await page.waitForTimeout(1000);
}

export async function adminEditProduct(admin: { page: Page }, product: string, field: RegExp, value: string, shown = value) {
  const page = admin.page;
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Products' }).first().click();
  await showAllRows(page);
  await apiHelper.triggerAndWaitWithRegex(page, page.getByRole('gridcell', { name: product, exact: true }), /.*~get$/);
  await typeInto(page, page, field, value, shown);
  await Promise.all([
    page.waitForResponse((r) => r.url().includes('/api/webshop/Admin') && r.request().method() !== 'GET'),
    page.getByRole('button', { name: /Update/ }).click(),
  ]);
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: /Update/ })).toBeHidden();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: /Back/ }).click();
}

async function distinctColumns(page: Page): Promise<number> {
  const xs = await page.getByTestId('product-card').evaluateAll((els) => els.map((e) => Math.round(e.getBoundingClientRect().left)));
  return new Set(xs).size;
}

// A second browser window on the back office, for checks that need an admin to change data (F15).
export async function adminPage(browser: Browser) {
  const context = await browser.newContext({ baseURL: ADMIN_URL, viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 } });
  const page = await context.newPage();
  await page.goto(ADMIN_URL); // app start URL, redirects to Keycloak
  await expect(page.locator('#kc-form-login')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
  await page.locator('#username').fill('admin');
  await page.locator('#password').fill('admin');
  await page.locator('#kc-login').click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Products' }).first()).toBeVisible({ timeout: 30000 });
  return { page, context };
}

async function readKey(page: Page, email: string): Promise<string> {
  await btn(page, /Refresh/).first().click().catch(() => undefined);
  const row = dataRows(page).filter({ hasText: email });
  await expect(row.first()).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
  const key = ((await row.first().innerText()).match(/[A-Z2-9]{32}/) ?? [])[0];
  expect(key, 'the invitation key is shown in the table').toBeTruthy();
  return key as string;
}

async function adminEditTiers(page: Page, product: string, tiers: Array<[string, string]>) {
  await clickMenu(page, 'Products');
  await showAllRows(page);
  await apiHelper.triggerAndWaitWithRegex(page, cell(page, product), /.*~get$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole('textbox', { name: /^Name/ })).toHaveValue(product);
  for (const [from, pct] of tiers) {
    if ((await cell(page, from).count()) > 0) continue; // already there (idempotent)
});

  ownerData: any;
  isOwnerLoading: boolean;
  actions: A;
  editMode?: boolean;
  storeDiff?: (attributeName: keyof T, value: any) => void;
  isFormUpdateable?: () => boolean;
}

async function placeOrder(page: Page, product = 'Day pack', quantity = 1): Promise<string> {
  if (quantity === 1) await addFromCard(page, product);
  else await addFromDetails(page, product, quantity);
  await openCart(page);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: /Check out/ }).click();
  const d = page.getByRole('dialog');
  await expect(d).toBeVisible();
  await typeInto(page, d, /Shipping address/, 'Lake Road 9, 5003 Bergen, Norway');
  await d.getByRole('button', { name: /(Ok|Submit)$/ }).click();
  await expect(numberOf(page)).toHaveValue(/^WS-\d{4}-\d{6}$/, { timeout: 20000 });
  return numberOf(page).inputValue();
}

async function saveView(page: Page) {
  await Promise.all([
    page.waitForResponse((r) => r.url().includes('/api/webshop/Admin') && r.request().method() !== 'GET'),
    page.getByRole('button', { name: /Update/ }).click(),
  ]);
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: /Update/ })).toBeHidden();
}

  await box.click();

});

import Box from '@mui/material/Box';
import IconButton from '@mui/material/IconButton';
import type { FC, ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { MdiIcon } from '~/components';

interface ScrollableMenuProps {
  children: ReactNode;
}

export const ScrollableMenu: FC<ScrollableMenuProps> = ({ children }) => {
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [showLeftButton, setShowLeftButton] = useState(false);
  const [showRightButton, setShowRightButton] = useState(false);

  const updateScrollButtons = useCallback(() => {
    const content: any = menuRef.current;
    setShowLeftButton(content.scrollLeft > 0);
    setShowRightButton(content.scrollLeft < content.scrollWidth - content.clientWidth);
  }, []);

  const scrollContent = useCallback((direction: number) => {
    const content: any = menuRef.current;
    const scrollAmount = 300;
    content.scrollLeft += direction * scrollAmount;
    window.setTimeout(() => {
      updateScrollButtons();
    }, 300);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    updateScrollButtons();
  }, []);

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        width: '100%',
      }}
    >
      <div style={{ flex: '0 0 auto', padding: '10px', marginRight: '5px' }}>
        <IconButton sx={{ visibility: !showLeftButton ? 'hidden' : 'visible' }} onClick={() => scrollContent(-1)}>
          <MdiIcon path="chevron-left" />
        </IconButton>

// Back office: change the return window (shop settings are ONE row).
async function setReturnWindow(a: Page, days: string) {
  await a.getByRole('button', { name: 'Shop settings' }).first().click();
  await expect(rows(a).first()).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
  await apiHelper.triggerAndWaitWithRegex(a, rows(a).first().getByRole('gridcell').nth(1), /.*~get$/);
  const box = field(a, /Return window/);
  await expect(box).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
  await box.click();
  await box.fill('');
  await box.pressSequentially(days, { delay: 5 });
  await box.blur();
  await a.waitForTimeout(700);
  await Promise.all([
    a.waitForResponse((r) => r.url().includes('/api/webshop/Admin') && r.request().method() !== 'GET'),
    btn(a, /Update/).click(),
  ]);
  await expect(btn(a, /Update/)).toBeHidden({ timeout: 15000 });
}

const customerReturnable = (page: Page) => rows(page);

async function join(page: Page, key: string) {
  await clickMenu(page, 'Join a company');
  await expect(page.getByRole('textbox', { name: /^Your e-mail/ })).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
  await btn(page, /Join with a key/).click();
  const d = page.getByRole('dialog');
  await expect(d).toBeVisible();
  await typeInto(page, d, /^Invitation key/, key);
  await d.getByRole('button', { name: /(Ok|Submit)$/ }).click();
}

async function companyInvite(page: Page, email: string, role: 'BUYER' | 'APPROVER'): Promise<string> {
  await clickMenu(page, 'My company');
  await expect(page.getByRole('textbox', { name: /^Company/ })).not.toHaveValue('', { timeout: 15000 });
  await btn(page, /Invite a colleague/).click();
  const d = page.getByRole('dialog');
  await expect(d).toBeVisible();
  await typeInto(page, d, /^E-mail of the colleague/, email);
  await d.getByRole('combobox', { name: /Role/ }).click();
  await page.getByRole('option', { name: role, exact: true }).click();
  await d.getByRole('button', { name: /(Ok|Submit)$/ }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('dialog')).toBeHidden({ timeout: 15000 });
  return readKey(page, email);
}

// ---- back office ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

async function adminOpenCompany(page: Page, name: string) {
  await clickMenu(page, 'Companies');
  await expect(dataRows(page).first()).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
  // the table keeps every company of every run and shows 10 rows per page: filter by the name of this test's company
  await openAddAndApplyFilters(page, apiHelper, [{ attributeName: 'Company', startOption: 'Like', option: 'Like', value: name, type: 'text' }]);
  await expect(page.getByRole('gridcell', { name, exact: true })).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
  await apiHelper.triggerAndWaitWithRegex(page, page.getByRole('gridcell', { name, exact: true }), /.*~get$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole('textbox', { name: /^Company/ })).toHaveValue(name);
}

async function openAdminOrder(page: Page, number: string) {
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'All orders', exact: true }).first().click();
  await expect(rows(page).first()).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
  // the table keeps every order of every run and shows 10 rows per page: filter by the number
  await openAddAndApplyFilters(page, apiHelper, [{ attributeName: 'Number', startOption: 'Like', option: 'Like', value: number, type: 'text' }]);
  const target = cell(page, number);
  await apiHelper.triggerAndWaitWithRegex(page, target, /.*~get$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole('textbox', { name: /^Number/ })).toHaveValue(number);
}

// paid, shipped, delivered: three real clicks of the admin
async function deliver(a: Page, number: string) {
  await openAdminOrder(a, number);
  await btn(a, 'Mark as paid').click();
  await expect(a.getByRole('textbox', { name: /^Status/ })).toHaveValue('Paid', { timeout: 15000 });
  await btn(a, 'Ship order').click();
  const sd = a.getByRole('dialog');
  await typeInto(a, sd, /Tracking number/, 'TRK-R12');

[7928] CALL bash: {"command": "cd ~/Projects/webshop-test/application/e2e/webshop__customer/playwright/tests; sed -n '/^export async function typeInto/,/^}/p' helpers-customer.ts", "timeout": 10}
[7929] TOOLRESULT: export async function typeInto(page: Page, scope: Page | ReturnType<Page['locator']>, label: RegExp, value: string, shown = value) {
  const box = scope.getByRole('textbox', { name: label });
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++) {
    await box.click();
    await box.fill('');
    await box.pressSequentially(value, { delay: 10 });
    await box.blur();
    await page.waitForTimeout(700); // DebouncedTextField
    if ((await box.inputValue()) === shown) return; // the generated field can drop keys while it re-renders: type again
  }

// F46: opening a notification marks it read. The documented post-refresh hook of the page calls the operation
// "markRead" (agent-docs/frontend/hooks/action-hooks.md). A write in a backend interceptor on the read was not kept.
const service = new CustomerServiceForNotificationsImpl(judoAxiosProvider);

const notificationHook: NotificationInfoNotificationInfo_View_EditActionsHook = () => ({
  postRefreshAction: async (data) => {
    if (data.read === false) {
      await service.markRead(data).catch(() => undefined); // the notification stays readable when marking fails
    }
  },
});

export function registerNotificationHook(context: BundleContext) {
  context.registerService<NotificationInfoNotificationInfo_View_EditActionsHook>(
    CUSTOMER_NOTIFICATIONS_ACCESS_VIEW_PAGE_ACTIONS_HOOK_INTERFACE_KEY,
    notificationHook,
  );
}

// Back office: an order from the list of all orders, newest first.
async function openAdminOrder(page: Page, number: string) {
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'All orders', exact: true }).first().click();
  await expect(rows(page).first()).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
  const target = cell(page, number);
  for (let i = 0; i < 2 && !(await target.isVisible()); i++) {
    await sortOrdered(page);
    await page.waitForTimeout(1200);
  }
  await apiHelper.triggerAndWaitWithRegex(page, target, /.*~get$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole('textbox', { name: /^Number/ })).toHaveValue(number);
}

[8044] TOOLRESULT: async function adminCreateCompany(page: Page, name: string, limit: string, poRequired: boolean) {
  await clickMenu(page, 'Companies');
  await apiHelper.triggerAndWaitWithRegex(page, btn(page, /Create/).first(), /.*template$/);
  const d = page.getByRole('dialog');
  await expect(d).toBeVisible();
  await typeInto(page, d, /^Company/, name);
  await typeInto(page, d, /^Credit limit/, limit, Number(limit).toLocaleString('en-US'));
  if (poRequired) await d.getByRole('checkbox', { name: /Purchase order number required/ }).check();
  await apiHelper.triggerAndWaitWithRegex(page, d.getByRole('button', { name: /Create$/ }).last(), /.*(list|~create)$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole('dialog')).toBeHidden();
}

async function adminAddCompanyPrice(page: Page, product: string, price: string) {
  await btn(page, /Create/).last().click(); // the prices table is the last table of the company view
  const d = page.getByRole('dialog');
  await expect(d).toBeVisible();
  await typeInto(page, d, /^Company price/, price);
  const combo = d.getByRole('combobox').first();
  await combo.click();
  await combo.pressSequentially(product, { delay: 20 });
  await page.getByText(product, { exact: true }).last().click(); // the suggestion list of the product link field
  await expect(combo).toHaveValue(product);
  await d.getByRole('button', { name: /(Ok|Create)$/ }).click();
  await expect(d).toBeHidden();
}

[8157] TOOLRESULT: 
async function backToShopAdmin(a: Admin) {
  await clickMenu(a.page, 'Dashboard');
}

async function clearRates(a: Admin) {
  await clickMenu(a.page, 'Exchange rates');
  await expect(a.page.getByText(/Rows per page/)).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
  for (let guard = 0; guard < 60; guard++) {
    const del = dataRows(a.page).first().getByRole('button', { name: /Delete/ });
    if ((await del.count()) === 0) break;
    await del.click();
    const confirm = a.page.getByRole('dialog').getByRole('button', { name: /(Ok|Delete|Yes|Confirm)$/ });
    if (await confirm.isVisible().catch(() => false)) await confirm.click();
    await a.page.waitForTimeout(1200);
  }
  await expect(emptyList(a.page)).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
}

The full customer Playwright run passes serially: 78 passed, 1 skipped (the old "empty state" skip). `--grep @smoke` passes (15), though the smoke run came before the last spec-only fixes, so I did not re-run it after them. The admin Playwright project (35 tests) does not pass: 18 pass and 17 fail (details below).

## Restart after the reboot
Docker, Postgres, Keycloak and the backend were restarted, then Vite for dev work. All data was kept. During the final runs Docker's disk went read-only. I restarted Docker Desktop, Postgres recovered by itself, and nothing was lost (602 orders afterwards).

## Screens converted
**Customer: 10 lists as cards through the hook**
- Products, orders, returns, return an item, notifications, addresses, quotes, seller products, my sales, exchange rates.
- The home grid and the product list share one `ProductCard`.

**Customer: custom visual elements**
- 8 detail views as a summary header plus sections: order, quote, sale, return, wallet, seller shop, company, notification. This marks 93 field elements `customImplementation` in the model.
- 11 relation lists as cards: order lines, returns, shipments, reviews, gift cards, points history, quote lines, members, invitations, seller ledger, sale lines.
- The home page is the existing custom page component.
- The top bar was tuned: nav now fits at 1440; at 1280 the last group ("Sell") is behind the scroll arrow.

**Admin: 6 screens**
- The dashboard has KPI tiles for today's figures and work waiting (to ship, returns, low stock, open quotes). Each tile opens its queue.
- Order queue ("Orders (30 days)" and "All orders") as cards, with Export CSV kept.
- Return-request queue as cards with Approve and Reject.
- Quotes as cards.
- Order, return and quote detail summaries. The quote discount field stays editable.

**Shared look in both apps:** `ShopCard`, status badges, `ListBars` (search, sort, create, refresh, loading and empty states) and `DetailLayout`.

## Behaviour kept
Two conversions lost actions in the first version and were fixed:
- The profile addresses stay a generated table again (Create and delete), see R16U-12.
- The cards now carry delete, remove and the admin Approve/Reject and "Request return" actions.

## Screens left generated
- Profile form and its addresses table (an editing form).
- All operation input forms, so validation and error messages stay unchanged.
- Customer shop settings view.
- Admin plain CRUD: products, categories, coupons, settings, admins, companies, sellers, sub-orders, exchange rates, imported products, audit log.
- Low stock, which shares the product table with the product CRUD.
- There is no review moderation screen in the model; the return-request queue took its place (R16U-9).
